# Card Index

2027 cards. Generated by tools/knowledge/build_index.py — do not edit by hand.
Card files live in `.knowledge/cards/` as `{YYYYMMDD}-{HHMM}-{ID}.md`.
Resolve a bare card ID by globbing `.knowledge/cards/*-<ID>.md`.
